Church Ladies\u003c/p\u003e \n  \u003cp style=\"text-align: justify;\"\u003eA group of women contemplate violence after they’re sent into foreign territory to make husbands of the enemy. A support network of traumatized joggers meets to discuss the bodies they’ve found on their runs. And a town replaces its Confederate monument with a rotating cast of local residents. Slippery but muscular, sly but electric, this stunning debut collection moves from horror to magical realism to satire with total authority. In these stories, characters build and remake their sense of home, be it with one another or within themselves.\u003c/p\u003e \n  \u003cp style=\"text-align: justify;\"\u003eAs in the very best collections, each of these stories is a world all its own, with a novel’s emotional heft and a poem’s laser focus on the most achingly resonant details of its characters’ lives. dazzling wit, subtle social criticism, sumptuous settings and the theme of a guilty secret proved a winner, both here and in his next three plays, A Woman of No Importance, An Ideal Husband, and his undisputed masterpiece, The Importance of Being Earnest.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eThis volume includes all Wilde's plays from his early tragedy Vera to the controversial Salome and the little known fragments, La Sainte Courtisane and A Florentine Tragedy. The edition affords a rare chance to see Wilde's best known work in the context of his entire dramatic output, and to appreciate plays which have hitherto received scant critical attention. Wilde’s plays have never failed to delight audiences and are a lasting testimony to their author’s supreme wit and theatrical genius.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp\u003e---------------------------------\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eOscar Wilde đã gây chấn động London với vở hài kịch đầu tay, Lady Windermere's Fan. Sự kết hợp giữa sự dí dỏm xuất sắc, phê phán xã hội tinh tế, bối cảnh xa hoa và chủ đề về một bí mật tội lỗi đã chứng tỏ sức hút tuyệt vời, cả trong vở kịch này lẫn ba vở kịch tiếp theo của ông, A Woman of No Importance, An Ideal Husband, và kiệt tác không thể chối cãi, The Importance of Being Earnest.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eTập ​​sách này bao gồm tất cả các vở kịch của Wilde, từ vở bi kịch đầu tay Vera đến vở Salome gây tranh cãi và những đoạn trích ít được biết đến, La Sainte Courtisane và A Florentine Tragedy. Ấn bản này mang đến một cơ hội hiếm hoi để xem xét tác phẩm nổi tiếng nhất của Wilde trong bối cảnh toàn bộ sự nghiệp sân khấu của ông, và để chiêm ngưỡng những vở kịch mà trước đây ít được giới phê bình chú ý. Các vở kịch của Wilde chưa bao giờ làm khán giả thất vọng và là minh chứng lâu dài cho trí tuệ siêu việt và thiên tài sân khấu của tác giả.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eGiá sản phẩm trên Tiki đã bao gồm thuế theo luật hiện hành. The supernatural and the mundane, the illusory and the substantial, are all shimmeringly blended. Rings.\u003c/p\u003e \n  \u003cp style=\"text-align:justify\"\u003eThe seven 'essays' by J.R.R. Tolkien assembled in this new paperback edition were with one exception red as general lectures on particular occasions; and while they mostly arose out of Tolkien's work in medieval literature, they are accessible to all. Two of them are concerned with Beowulf, including the well-known lecture whose title is taken for this book, and one with Sir Gawain and the Green Knight, given in the University of Glasgow in 1953.\u003c/p\u003e \n  \u003cp style=\"text-align:justify\"\u003eAlso included in this volume is the lecture English and Welsh; the Valedictory Address to the University of Oxford in 1959; and a paper on Invented Languages red in 1931, with exemplification from poems in the Elvish tongues. Most famous of all is On Fairy-Stories, a discussion of the nature of fairy-tales and fantasy, which gives insight into Tolkien's approach to the whole genre.\u003c/p\u003e \n  \u003cp style=\"text-align:justify\"\u003eThe pieces in this collection cover a period of nearly thirty years, beginning six years before the publication of The Hobbit, with a unique 'academic' lecture on his invention (calling it A Secret Vice) and concluding with his farewell to professorship, five years after the publication of The Lord of the Rings.\u003c/p\u003e\n \u003c/body\u003e\n\u003c/html\u003e\u003cp\u003eGiá sản phẩm trên Tiki đã bao gồm thuế theo luật hiện hành.
